Their common responsibility is the exchange context, including routing and delivery state, rather than the business meaning of every payload. This is a broader frame or enabling context, not proof of semantic equivalence to the proposed part. Its explicit boundary is: Information administration and coordination preserve the authority of the responsible source, clinical actor and governed domain; they confer no additional real-world management authority. Recommend retaining this bounded concept, subject to SA review of the compared scopes and exact graph below. - [[CT2] Provenance & Traceability](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/68a0755aa4e14a509ac5e6c0bbcff3e7.md) — `68a0755aa4e14a509ac5e6c0bbcff3e7`; {"semantic": "Canonical", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Canonical", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: - **Architectural Scope**: Preserve required origin attribution, meaningful transformation history, significant execution checkpoints, tamper-evident audit evidence and end-to-end traceability. Harmonia SHALL preserve provenance, audit and operational evidence where required by governing obligations, architectural significance, accountability, reconstruction, security or assurance needs. Routine technical activity SHALL NOT automatically require durable evidentiary recording.
- **Functional Responsibilities**:
  - Recording attributable, verifiable origin metadata for information sources, authors, and timestamps.
  - Tracking information-significant transformation history and required mapping checkpoints as information crosses system seams.
  - Capturing non-PHI operational and security audit trails for regulatory compliance (HIPAA, GDPR).
  - Supporting end-to-end transaction tracing across asynchronous processing pipelines.
- **Collaborative Nature**: Participating elements preserve required provenance and audit context according to policy. Telemetry, operational facts, provenance, audit and assurance evidence remain distinct. Being evidence is contextual, not an intrinsic property of every recorded fact; preservation does not establish an assurance conclusion.
  Boundary: Telemetry, provenance, audit and assurance evidence remain distinct. Recording evidence neither establishes an assurance conclusion nor automatically requires retention of routine technical activity.

**Assessment and rationale:** OVERLAPPING. Content attribution/transformation lineage is an information responsibility. The EC lineage ability enables it, and the Feature contributes bounded transformation evidence. DR-18/19 preserve provenance management separately from audit evidence and assurance conclusions. Shared provenance meaning warrants coordinated abstraction review, not identity merger.

**Proposed treatment:** ACCEPT each bounded concept; reconcile the layering of existing Data Provenance Capture, EC lineage and transformation Feature.

**Consequences if authorised:** Confirm existing proposed containment and responsibility representation against accepted DR-18/19; preserve the client-library/component and information definitions without modifying them.

Definition: Clinical Device Administration represents device types, identifiable instances, patient or location associations and communication endpoints. This lets device-originated information be interpreted in relation to the actual device and care context, rather than merely a network address. Type, instance, assignment and connectivity each answer a different question about the source of device data.
  Boundary: Information administration does not confer authority over the represented person, organisation, service, place, device or clinical activity.
- [[CT2] Clinical Technology Management](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/6c91476a45eb4432b1f90b47a4c2ad35.md) — `6c91476a45eb4432b1f90b47a4c2ad35`; {"semantic": "Canonical", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Established", "structural_id_status": "Established", "name_status": "Canonical", "ancestry_status": "established"}. Definition: Manage clinical technology as a coherent business concern rather than only the device information Harmonia happens to use.

Type, instance, assignment and connectivity each answer a different question about the source of device data. Its explicit boundary is: Information administration and coordination preserve the authority of the responsible source, clinical actor and governed domain; they confer no additional real-world management authority. Recommend retaining this bounded concept, subject to SA review of the compared scopes and exact graph below. No evidence cited here establishes an additional same-instance equivalent; this is not an exhaustive uniqueness or gap finding. AX-14/17/18 and DR-6 permit layered and partial contributions.

- [[CT2] Clinical Knowledge Services](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/bbd2bd21859f40e39963bdbe7a851686.md) — `bbd2bd21859f40e39963bdbe7a851686`; {"semantic": "Canonical", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Canonical", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: Administer clinical knowledge requests, distribute governed knowledge artefacts and administer approved reference datasets.
  Boundary: Information administration does not confer authority over the represented person, organisation, service, place, device or clinical activity.
- [[CT2] Semantic Governance & Conformance](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/9ee41437bd4c4bd18389f05c12108f97.md) — `9ee41437bd4c4bd18389f05c12108f97`; {"semantic": "Canonical", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Canonical", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: - **Architectural Scope**: Govern canonical models, schemas, terminologies, value sets, mapping tables, constraint rules, and semantic conformance validation across the enterprise.
- **Functional Responsibilities**:
  - Defining and publishing canonical healthcare data models and profile structures.
  - Governing clinical terminology mappings, value sets, and concept lookups (SNOMED CT, LOINC).
  - Validating inbound and outbound message representations against published semantic rules.
  - Governing semantic versioning, deprecation, and backward compatibility across schema revisions.
- **Component Relationship**: Informs the Calliope strategic logical component, but Calliope is the responsibility centre, not the capability definition.
  Boundary: Conformance does not establish clinical truth, information authority or disclosure permission. Calliope is an established responsibility centre, not the definition of the capability.
- [[CT3] Active Information Availability](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/bb711f4d8fb74af2917869b9b05f7f84.md) — `bb711f4d8fb74af2917869b9b05f7f84`; {"semantic": "Proposed", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Proposed", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: Maintain responsive, reconstructable active information and coordinate its safe use.
  Boundary: An active generation is not an authoritative version; failure to converge does not establish valid active state.
  Independent adjudication: P1-332 (preserved source locator `05-current-unresolved-enterprise.md#p1-332`).
- [[CT2] Information Authority Qualification](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/cbba9984dd404deabed41fdcc96088b1.md) — `cbba9984dd404deabed41fdcc96088b1`; {"semantic": "Proposed", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Proposed", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: Represent and qualify the source-authority and credibility standing of information independently of transport, persistence and concurrency.
  Boundary: This is not independent service assurance, a universal conflict arbiter or clinical truth determination. Existing EC-13 governs authority nomenclature; this capability applies and preserves the qualified standing.
  Independent adjudication: P1-357 (preserved source locator `05-current-unresolved-enterprise.md#p1-357`).
- [[CT2] Provenance & Traceability](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/68a0755aa4e14a509ac5e6c0bbcff3e7.md) — `68a0755aa4e14a509ac5e6c0bbcff3e7`; {"semantic": "Canonical", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Canonical", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: - **Architectural Scope**: Preserve required origin attribution, meaningful transformation history, significant execution checkpoints, tamper-evident audit evidence and end-to-end traceability. Harmonia SHALL preserve provenance, audit and operational evidence where required by governing obligations, architectural significance, accountability, reconstruction, security or assurance needs. Routine technical activity SHALL NOT automatically require durable evidentiary recording.
- **Functional Responsibilities**:
  - Recording attributable, verifiable origin metadata for information sources, authors, and timestamps.
  - Tracking information-significant transformation history and required mapping checkpoints as information crosses system seams.
  - Capturing non-PHI operational and security audit trails for regulatory compliance (HIPAA, GDPR).
  - Supporting end-to-end transaction tracing across asynchronous processing pipelines.
- **Collaborative Nature**: Participating elements preserve required provenance and audit context according to policy. Telemetry, operational facts, provenance, audit and assurance evidence remain distinct. Being evidence is contextual, not an intrinsic property of every recorded fact; preservation does not establish an assurance conclusion.
  Boundary: Telemetry, provenance, audit and assurance evidence remain distinct. Recording evidence neither establishes an assurance conclusion nor automatically requires retention of routine technical activity.
- [[CT3] Authoritative Durable State Establishment](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/0d5bd68ff50f410aaceed05377e1a4df.md) — `0d5bd68ff50f410aaceed05377e1a4df`; {"semantic": "Proposed", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Proposed", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: Atomically establish durable managed state and its authoritative version progression.
  Boundary: Commit is distinct from semantic source authority and from successful active convergence; applications do not gain a raw persistence path.
  Independent adjudication: P1-019 (preserved source locator `05-current-unresolved-enterprise.md#p1-019`).
- [[CT2] Managed State & Lifecycle](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/6296abeee5074b9e80f21aea3a297e42.md) — `6296abeee5074b9e80f21aea3a297e42`; {"semantic": "Canonical", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Canonical", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: - **Architectural Scope**: Govern the current and historical state, effective temporal periods, state transition validations, and lifecycle progression of managed integration artifacts and entities.
- **Functional Responsibilities**:
  - Defining and enforcing valid state transition state machines (e.g., `REQUESTED` $\to$ `VALIDATED` $\to$ `IN_PROGRESS` $\to$ `COMPLETED`).
  - Maintaining temporal validity intervals (effective start and end dates) for relationships, credentials, and states.
  - Supporting non-destructive state progression, preserving historical state versions for longitudinal review.
  - Rejecting illegal or out-of-order state transitions while accommodating idempotent replay.
- **Anti-Responsibilities**: Does not define thread-level mutexes or database transaction isolation levels.
  Boundary: Business lifecycle integrity does not prescribe locks, database isolation or worker implementation, and illustrative state names do not define every subject's transitions.
- [[CT2] Clinical Knowledge Management](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/76b375248aee49aeb5a5044176a688d8.md) — `76b375248aee49aeb5a5044176a688d8`; {"semantic": "Canonical", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Established", "structural_id_status": "Established", "name_status": "Canonical", "ancestry_status": "established"}. Definition: Identify, extract, formulate, associate, govern and share reusable clinical knowledge.
  Boundary: Publication does not establish applicability to an individual or a clinical decision. Concept association does not redefine terminology, and formulation does not constitute approval.

**Assessment and rationale:** OVERLAPPING. The existing proposal bounds an enduring ability as follows: Administer approved runtime reference-dataset content and revisions separately from the reusable models defining its meaning. The source frame [CT2] Clinical Knowledge Services concerns: Administer clinical knowledge requests, distribute governed knowledge artefacts and administer approved reference datasets. This is a broader frame or enabling context, not proof of semantic equivalence to the proposed part. Its explicit boundary is: Does not own external ontology authority, execute authorisation policy or establish durable truth independently of the normal persistence boundary. Recommend retaining this bounded concept, subject to SA review of the compared scopes and exact graph below. No evidence cited here establishes an additional same-instance equivalent; this is not an exhaustive uniqueness or gap finding. AX-14/17/18 and DR-6 permit layered and partial contributions.

**Proposed treatment:** ACCEPT the existing bounded architectural concept under its present canonical identity; retain or reconcile its current representation only as the SA decides. Preserve its Proposed standing until that decision is incorporated.

**Consequences if authorised:** SA must affirm or amend the existing definition and proposed CT3 framing under [CT2] Clinical Knowledge Services. Preserve BC/BEC/EC responsibility distinctions. Establish any changed Composition, Aggregation, Realization, Home or responsibility assertion separately; no formal names or identities are invented.

These contributions are complementary: a responsive working view must not become the authority for durable history.
  Boundary: Information administration does not confer authority over the represented person, organisation, service, place, device or clinical activity.

The existing proposal bounds an enduring ability as follows: Curate associations to information and binary content needed to interpret and use a clinical record, retaining custody and source-authority distinctions. The source frame [CT2] Patient Clinical Record concerns: Patient Clinical Record provides a governed longitudinal representation across encounters, providers and source systems. Assembly supplies the timeline, active access supports current care context, and durable preservation retains the history of what Harmonia received, knew or managed. These contributions are complementary: a responsive working view must not become the authority for durable history. Persistence authority, source clinical meaning and external repository authority remain separate. Recommend retaining this bounded concept, subject to SA review of the compared scopes and exact graph below. No evidence cited here establishes an additional same-instance equivalent; this is not an exhaustive uniqueness or gap finding. AX-14/17/18 and DR-6 permit layered and partial contributions.

- [[CT2] On-Call Management](../../../../../../../continuum/strategy/capability/b16cef8f2bfd4b92948ec75cbcfa7b9a.md) — `b16cef8f2bfd4b92948ec75cbcfa7b9a`; {"semantic": "Canonical", "currentness": "current", "placement_status": "Proposed", "structural_id_status": "Proposed", "name_status": "Canonical", "ancestry_status": "proposed"}. Definition: On-Call Management resolves the active provider for a specialty, service or hospital at the relevant time. It turns changing coverage information into usable routing context so that operational communication can reach the intended capacity. This differs from authoring the roster or deciding who is professionally qualified for the requested activity.
  Boundary: Information administration does not confer authority over the represented person, organisation, service, place, device or clinical activity.

**Assessment and rationale:** OVERLAPPING.
